## Iter-255 (2026-06) — Security audit round 15 (P0-01, P1-01/02/03/04, P2-01/02; P1-05/06/07 + P2-03/04/05 recorded)
- **P0-01 fenced exactly-once** — `nl_execution.py`: integer `execution.fence` (+1 on every reclaim), every receipt write / action start / lease renewal / finalization guarded by (id, owner, fence, live lease); `renew_lease` before each action; `reserve_effect` reserves the idempotency key in `nl_effects` (unique `_id`) right before the side effect; `execute_one(user_id, act, idem_key)` propagates it (trigger insert dedup via `idem_key`, trade/bot writes conditional). A stale worker returns `lease_lost` (409 execution_in_progress on confirm; trigger sweeper returns early). Tests `test_r15_fenced_execution.py`: stale-A-resumes-after-B-reclaim (proposals AND triggers), 3-worker race, effect boundary.
- **P1-01 typed schemas** — `nl_actions.py` Pydantic discriminated union: finite thresholds 0<x≤50, conditions drop|rise|move, targets all|high_risk|SYMBOL, risk levels enum, ≤8 actions, ≤3 nested, no recursive triggers; applied in `nl_command` before preview/storage. `_move_stops_breakeven` is monotonic (`breakeven_decision`: BUY stop only up, SELL only down, never wrong side of market, idempotent); `_set_risk_level` raises; unknown types raise → receipts `failed`, never `done`.
- **P1-02 public readiness** — `/api/status` availability-only: `trading.attestation {attested, basis, input_version, inventory_hash(12), as_of}` from `inventory_projection.projection()` (declared expectation + approved hash == current + zero violations/structural defects) AND every intended enabled account READY under one unchanged `authority_version`; counts/sig/fresh_terminals removed; `new_exposure_allowed` false unless attested.
- **P1-03 ledger** — per-issuer key registry `STATEMENT_ATTESTATION_KEYS_JSON` (fallback single pinned key), `key_id` stored; statement currency must equal account base currency; `platform_totals(currency=)` never sums foreign-currency deals/flows (`MIXED_CURRENCY` status); rows keep `statement_raw` + `broker_login`; same-id/different-hash race → 409; `verify_chain` + `ledger_gate` re-verify signature/key mapping, identity, currency, chain roots + HMAC, continuous coverage at PUBLICATION time (reasons STATEMENT_SIGNATURE_INVALID / UNVERIFIABLE / KEY_ROTATED / IDENTITY_CHANGED / CURRENCY_MISMATCH / COVERAGE_GAP / LEDGER_CHAIN_BROKEN); `verified_ledger` root embedded in the attestation payload.
- **P1-04 model governance** — approval reservation `(user_id, digest, principal_id)` unique, `pending`→`committed`, released on failure, stale pending reclaimable; outbox publication `claimed`→`published` with lease; only `published` clears the outbox. Tests inject failures after each write.
- **P2-01** — `campaign_trend` projects service/host/pid/build; per-instance daily medians → fleet daily MAX; `instances`, `restarts`, `fleet_rule` reported; sampler emits a sample 5 s after start. **P2-02** — `email_verified is True` required (and `/api/auth/me` now uses the same rule; admins backfilled), Telegram one-time chat handshake (`/notifications/telegram/verify/start|confirm`, `telegram_verified`, reset on rebinding), role-based step-up (`ALERT_TEST_STEP_UP_ROLES` + MFA users); UI `TelegramVerify` block.
- Open findings recorded (r15): P1-05 authoritative release artifact, P1-06 publication order, P1-07 live 6/3/3 evidence, P2-05 staging parity (+ P2-03 charts, P2-04 fingerprint from r14). Verdict stays NOT RELEASABLE until produced on a promoted artifact.

## Iter-259 (2026-06) — Security audit round 16 (P0-01, P1-01/02/04/06, P2-05/06 fixed; P1-03/05 + P2-01/02/03/04 recorded) — testing agent iteration_213: 100%
- **P0-01 fence reaches the side effect** — `nl_execution.py`: `nl_effects` is a fenced state machine `reserved → dispatching → applying → completed|failed|uncertain` (owner, fence, execution_id, decision_id, authority_version). `effect_context()` is passed to `execute_one(..., ctx=)`; every ultimate handler (`_disable_bots/_enable_bots/_move_stops_breakeven/_close_all_trades/_set_risk_level/_save_trigger/PANIC`) calls `apply_effect(ctx)` (CAS dispatching→applying under owner+fence, raises LeaseLost) immediately before its domain write and stamps rows with `nl_effect{key,fence,execution_id,decision_id,authority_version}`; CLOSE rows carry `close_idem_key/close_fence`, forwarded by `bridge_routes` dispatch and `panic_routes` (`stamp=`). Before each effect: lease renew (guard now includes `status: executing` ⇒ cancelled proposal stops), `recheck_authority()` (fresh `decide_user`; risk-increasing refused when exposure disallowed OR `input_version` moved since confirmation). Recovery consults the row: not yet applying → takeover + run under new fence; completed/failed → outcome copied (`recovered: True`); applying → `uncertain`, fenced so the stale completion fails. Tests rewritten: stale worker executes NOTHING; mid-write → uncertain/never replayed; authority recheck; real handlers refuse stale fence; r14 crash test now recovers to `executed`.
- **P1-01** — `nl_actions.py` action-specific targets: bot actions `all|high_risk|bot:<24hex>`, trade actions `all|SYMBOL`, PANIC/trigger `target` fixed `all`; `nl_preview.bot_query/trade_query` refuse anything else (a symbol can never widen to all bots); previews carry `resolved_ids` + `inventory_hash`; `_set_risk_level` honours scope.
- **P1-02** — `broker_statement_ledger.coverage_report()` merges RECONCILED intervals clipped to `[as_of-STATEMENT_COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS(30), as_of]`: reasons STATEMENT_COVERAGE_START / _GAP / _STALE (freshness `STATEMENT_COVERAGE_FRESHNESS_H`, default 7 d); `ledger_snapshot()` embeds per-account window_start/as_of/coverage_pct/intervals(+hash)/currency/broker_login/issuer/key_id + formulas into the attestation payload.
- **P1-04** — `release.yml`: pip-audit + grype + signer canary (HARD fail) run on the LOCAL image before GHCR login; images pushed to `quarantine-<sha>` tags (digest captured); after the signed attestation + evidence record, the public `${IMG}:${REF}` tag is attached EXACTLY ONCE via `docker buildx imagetools create` (refused if it already exists). Test updated.
- **P1-06** — `/api/status.overall` downgraded to `degraded` when EA bridge ≠ operational or trading not ready; StatusPage banner "Platform available · trading ready/degraded" (no "All systems operational").
- **P2-05** — `deploy_watch._notify` uses unique outbox `deploy_watch_outbox` `(watch_id:kind)` claimed→sent|failed with provider id; retries deduped. **P2-06** — Telegram MarkdownV2 bodies are raw-string module constants (`TELEGRAM_TEST_TEXT`, `TELEGRAM_VERIFY_TEXT`); compile with `-W error` clean; rendered-message test.
- Recorded in `docs/open_findings.json` (r16): P1-03 authoritative release identities, P1-05 live capital-safety acceptance, P2-01 chart provenance, P2-02 fingerprint completeness, P2-03 Turnstile matrix, P2-04 ingress mTLS. Verdict stays NOT RELEASABLE until produced on a promoted artifact.
- Tests: `tests/integration/test_r16_audit.py` (7), r15 suite 15, critical_controls 55, integration 134, unit 666; testing agent iteration_213 100% (live confirm flow: receipts carry decision_id/authority_version, rows stamped, replay 409). Manifest 4,432 / 446 files; preflight 5/5; secret scan clean.

- **P0-01 destination-enforced fence** — `nl_execution.fenced(db, ctx, work)`: CAS `dispatching→applying`, the handler's domain writes (`work(session)`) and `→completed` commit in ONE MongoDB transaction (replica set); a stale worker aborts inside the transaction and writes nothing. ## Iter-265 (2026-06) — Security audit round 21 (deployed app) → PASS; P3 hardenings applied

## Iter-266 (2026-06) — Security audit round 22 (deployed app) → PASS; four P3 hardenings applied
- Auditor (read-only) covered pairing/installation trust chain, bridge token lifecycle, trade ingestion, admin/ops/metrics, auth/TOTP/step-up/reset, WebSocket, frontend sinks, live preview probes. No P0/P1/P2.
- **P3-1 TOTP replay** — `totp.verify_code_once(db, user_id, secret, code)`: used-code cache `totp_used` (TTL 180 s) refuses a second redemption of the same code; wired into login 2FA, 2FA disable and step-up (`auth_routes`).
- **P3-2 one-time e-mail tokens hashed at rest** — `security.token_digest` (SHA-256); `activation_token_sha256` / `password_reset_token_sha256` stored, lookups by digest, plaintext fields unset (register, resend, forgot, verify, reset, seed force-reset). Legacy plaintext column never redeems.
- **P3-3 user_trust proof labelled** — heartbeat stores `ea_binary_sha256_method` (installation method); `live_gate` → `EA_BINARY_PROOF_UNATTESTED` for `user_trust` (installer-paired only counts as proof).
- **P3-4 throttles** — bridge router dependency `_bridge_ip_throttle` (per-IP `BRIDGE_IP_LIMIT_PER_MIN`, default 1200/min, bypass header honoured outside production); `/setup/claim-pairing` 20/10 min per IP.
- Live: production still OLD build (health 500). Preview: reset/verify invalid → 400, bridge bad token → 401, claim → 400, no 500s.
- Tests: `tests/integration/test_r22_audit.py` (4); critical_controls 81 (83/83 on local rs0, 0 skips), integration 140 (+scalp live-only), unit 661. Manifest regenerated; rc_lock refrozen; consistency OK; secret scan + gitleaks clean.

## Iter-269 (2026-06) — Publish failure ROOT CAUSE fixed (logs obtained)
- Log: `RuntimeError: release signer configuration invalid: APP_ENV=production forbids RELEASE_SIGNER=local` → uvicorn exits → k8s readiness timeout → "Publishing failed" (every publish since the round-8/9 signer rule). Production Secrets hold RELEASE_SIGNER=local + RELEASE_SIGNER_ALLOW_LOCAL_IN_PROD=true and the panel refuses edits.
- Fix 1 — **deferred signer mode**: new key `RELEASE_SIGNER_DEFERRED=true` (backend/.env → flows into Secrets on republish; honoured only in production; a fully configured `RELEASE_SIGNER=external` always wins). `release_signing._mode()` → "deferred": boot passes (`signer_config_violations` only demands the private key be absent — satisfied via PRODUCTION_RETIRED_SECRETS), `sign_hex`/`public_key_b64` raise `SignerDeferred` (nothing unsigned is ever emitted), `signer_health` ok=False deferred=True, `deferred_gate()` → `RELEASE_SIGNER_DEFERRED` CLOSE_ONLY wired into `trading_authority.infrastructure_domain` and bot activation; preflight shows WARN; `/api/release-key` returns public_key_b64=null instead of 500.
- Fix 2 — **second production trap** found by simulating the boot with the logged Secrets: `modules/pamm/strategy_guard._enforce_production_provenance` raised `STOIC_IMAGE_DIGEST missing` at first import (lazy import inside `/api/health` → the 500 the OLD live build shows). Managed publish injects no image digest/Git SHA, so production now derives `source-sha256:<tree digest>` (`source_tree_digest()`, 483 files, ~20 ms), publishes it via `STOIC_IMAGE_DIGEST` env for all consumers, `PROVENANCE_KIND=source-tree`; the guard is imported in `on_startup` so it fails at boot, never at request time. Injected image digest without SHA is still refused.
- Fix 3 — `TURNSTILE_EXPECTED_HOSTNAMES` added to .env (prod + preview hosts) so a Turnstile-enabled prod DB cannot fail the hostname-binding boot check.
- Verified: production boot simulation (uvicorn :8011 with APP_ENV=production, RELEASE_SIGNER=local, ALLOW_LOCAL=true) → startup complete, /health 200, /api/health 200 (provenance source-tree), /api/health/ready 200, /api/release-key 200 deferred, /api/status 200, 0 tracebacks. Unit 731 (new test_deferred_signer.py 5), critical_controls 85, integration 142, turnstile suites 57, legacy signer/preflight/provenance tests adapted (delenv the new flags). Pre-existing unrelated failure: test_iter181 all_good_is_ready (local standalone Mongo).
- Operator next: republish → live_probe; later provision Fly signer and flip RELEASE_SIGNER_DEFERRED=false (docs/open_findings.json OPS-r24-*).

## Iter-278 (2026-06) — Installer lock after successful install
- User: "After a successful installation, the installer is to be locked or deleted." Chosen: LOCK (update.sh stays open).
- `bootstrap.sh` writes `<project>/.stoic-installed` (installed_at/mode/domain/commit/host; chmod 444 + `chattr +i` where supported) after success (before the signed report so `installer_locked=true` is recorded). While present, `bootstrap.sh` and `install.sh` exit 3 with the lock content + pointers to `deploy/update.sh`. `--check-only` always allowed (never touches the lock). `--unlock` removes the lock only after the system check is green, re-locks on success, and re-locks in rollback (HAD_LOCK). install.sh honours `STOIC_INSTALL_UNLOCK=1` (exported by bootstrap) or `--unlock`. doctor: `installer: LOCKED since …` PASS / WARN in production when unlocked. Docs updated. Tests 16/16 (behavioural: exit 3 both scripts, env override, check-only keeps lock). Locks regenerated.

## Iter-283 (2026-06) — Server run #4: mongo restart loop "Permission denied"
- First real Docker run of the stack. Everything built/started; mongo looped: `docker-entrypoint.sh: line 83: /run/secrets/mongo_root_password: Permission denied` — the official image gosu-drops to `mongodb` BEFORE file_env reads the root password (and before init js / keyFile), while host secrets are root-only 0600. Only mongo affected (all our images run as root).
- Fix: `deploy/mongo-start.sh` (root) stages mongo_keyfile/mongo_root_password/mongo_app_password into `/data/configdb/secrets` (400, mongodb-owned, anonymous volume), exports `MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_PASSWORD_FILE` + new `MONGO_APP_PASSWORD_FILE` (honoured by mongo-init.js, fallback to /run/secrets) and passes `--keyFile "$S/mongo_keyfile"`. Simulated with a real `mongodb` user: staged copies readable, host secret not. Tests 17/17. r283. Recovery on server: `docker compose down -v` (fresh, no data) then re-run the one-liner.
- Server run #5: `docker compose down -v` left stoic-signer-1 "marked for removal" (RHEL8 overlay2 busy) → compose up failed "container is marked for removal and cannot be started" → rollback. Fix r284: `compose_up()` in lib.sh force-removes `--status removing --status dead` containers first (dockerd restart fallback). Removed obsolete `version:` from docker-compose.yml. User told to `docker rm -f stoic-signer-1; systemctl restart docker` then re-run.
- Server run #6 (r283 still — r284 not pushed): step 2 "updating /opt/stoic" failed "You are not currently on a branch" — rollback had left HEAD detached; `git checkout $(rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)` → "HEAD" + `pull --ff-only`. Fix r285: realign to remote default branch (`symbolic-ref origin/HEAD` → fallback main/master; `checkout -f -B <br> origin/<br>`), `|| true` on the symref pipeline (pipefail). Proven with a bare repo: detached → origin/main, both with and without origin/HEAD symref. stoic-signer-1 still "Dead" on host → r284 compose_up zombie removal must land (Save to GitHub).
- Server run #7 (r286): source update fixed (at origin/main 5e961b2), builds cached, but stoic-signer-1 "Removal In Progress" survived `docker rm -f` + dockerd restart + nsenter umount (mount leaked into another namespace — cPanel host). r287: compose_up last resort = collect MergedDirs → stop docker → umount -l → rm -rf /var/lib/docker/containers/<zombie-id> (zombie IDs only) → start docker. User given the same manual one-liner.

## Iter-292 (2026-06) — Server run #12: compose recreate → "Error while Removing" / name conflict
- r291 run: build OK, `compose up` stopped the old workers, renamed them `<12hex>_stoic-worker-*` and FAILED to remove 4 of them (overlay EBUSY) → `Conflict. The container name "/6dd45105ceb4_stoic-worker-tuning-1" is already in use` → rollback. Root cause: RHEL 8 default `fs.may_detach_mounts=0` + mounts leaked into cPanel CageFS/LVE / httpd PrivateTmp namespaces. The r284–r287 reaper only ran BEFORE `compose up`, so containers dying DURING recreate were never reaped.

Tests `tests/test_iter292_zombie_reaper.py` (5) replay the exact server failure against a fake docker shim. Manual unblock for the user: `sysctl -w fs.may_detach_mounts=1; docker ps -a --filter name=_stoic- -q | xargs -r docker rm -f` then re-run the one-liner.
- r293: DNS check (bootstrap step 0 + doctor.sh) treats an A record pointing at ANY IPv4 configured on the host (`ip -4 -o addr … scope global`) as "this host" — cPanel boxes carry the server main IP (173.231.199.42, egress seen by ipify) AND the account's dedicated IP (192.249.113.78 = stoicaibot.com A record, TTL 900). Previously WARNed as foreign.

## Iter-296 (2026-06) — Root cause of the EBUSY loop found on the host
- User's diagnostics: dockerd IS in the host mount ns; `fuser -m` shows no host holder; but 67 host processes in FOREIGN mount namespaces hold copies of the container overlay mounts — php-fpm ×43 (cPanel pools / CageFS), next-server ×24 (node app), mariadbd, named, chronyd, rsyslogd, systemd-udevd, systemd-resolve, imh-nrpe (systemd PrivateTmp/ProtectSystem units). Copies made at (re)start of those services pin `merged` → docker rmdir EBUSY.
- r296 lib.sh `detach_leaked_mounts()` (called at the top of `reap_zombies`, i.e. before every `compose up` pass): one pid per foreign mount ns (skips host ns and container cgroups) → `nsenter -m -t <pid> umount -l` every `/var/lib/docker/**` mount copy that is NOT a `shared:` peer (a peer umount would propagate back and kill a live container — those are counted and reported instead). `PROC_ROOT` override for tests; 8 tests green (fake /proc tree + fake nsenter). Experiment given to the user (alpine A/B with `systemctl restart chronyd` between) to confirm the mechanism.

## Iter-297 (2026-06) — A/B experiment: a 2-second-old container is ALREADY unremovable → propagation, not service restarts
- `docker rm -f` of a fresh alpine container fails EBUSY with no namespace created in between ⇒ container rootfs mounts are propagated INSTANTLY (`/` is `shared`, sandboxed services are slaves) into all 67 foreign namespaces, and the RHEL 8 kernel refuses rmdir while any copy exists.
- r297: bootstrap step 1 makes the Docker root a private mount (`mount --bind /var/lib/docker /var/lib/docker; mount --make-rprivate`) and persists it as `/etc/systemd/system/docker.service.d/10-stoic-private-root.conf` (ExecStartPre); step 0 PASS/WARN on `findmnt -no PROPAGATION`; doctor FAILs when not private (with the one-line fix). nsenter detach (r296) kept for copies made by later service restarts. Manual unblock given to the user (same two mount commands + alpine proof).
- r298: server run #15 showed the leaked copies are `shared:` PEERS (26) → detach skipped them all and fell through to metadata removal. Refined: a shared copy is skipped only when it belongs to a RUNNING container (rootfs MergedDir or containers/<id>/ path in `docker ps`); dead containers' peer copies are detached (umount propagates back and clears the host's stale mount — exactly what unblocks rmdir). Test updated (fake `docker ps -q` / inspect MergedDir).

## Iter-299 (2026-06) — Server run #16: STACK INSTALLED (compose up clean, infra green); two post-install fixes
- With the private Docker root the recreate went through in one pass. Doctor then: (1) `/var/lib/docker propagation is 'shared'` again — dockerd's `setupDaemonRootPropagation` flips a PRIVATE root back to shared on every start (it accepts shared or slave only) and the reaper had restarted dockerd; (2) `https://stoicaibot.com/api/health → 406` = cPanel ModSecurity default deny on the proxied request.
- r299: Docker root made a **slave** mount (`mount --make-rslave`; stops outward propagation, kept by dockerd) — bootstrap step 1 always writes the docker.service drop-in `10-stoic-private-root.conf` (ExecStartPre bind + make-rslave); step 0 / doctor accept anything not `shared`. cpanel.sh SSL include gets `<IfModule security2_module> SecRuleEngine Off </IfModule>`; 406 hint points at WHM ModSecurity Domain Manager. Manual unblock for the user: `mount --make-rslave /var/lib/docker` + re-run `deploy/proxy/cpanel.sh stoicaibot.com` + re-run bootstrap to lock.
- r300: cPanel include excludes the service shortcuts from the proxy (`ProxyPass /cpanel|/whm|/webmail|/cpanelwebcall|/autodiscover !`) — with `ProxyPass /` they were reaching the STOIC frontend (→ /welcome). Port URLs (:2083/:2087/:2096) always work regardless.

Now resolves to the vhost IP from `/var/cpanel/userdata/<user>/<domain>` (`ip:`), fallback 127.0.0.1. Doctor's domain probe uses real DNS (already the vhost IP).
- r302: doctor probes both `https://DOMAIN` and `https://www.DOMAIN` (/api/health): 200 PASS; proxy-200-but-bad-cert → WARN (AutoSSL); www without DNS → WARN; otherwise FAIL. Backend already allows www (CORS_ORIGINS/TURNSTILE_EXPECTED_HOSTNAMES set by install.sh), cPanel vhost has ServerAlias www.

## Iter-305 (2026-06) — why "slave" kept turning into private/shared
- `mount --make-rslave` on dockerd's self-bind → `findmnt` says **private**: that bind is a shared mount in a peer group of its own, so demoting it leaves no master → private → dockerd flips it to shared on next start. A real slave needs a master: bind from the plain dir (peer of `/`, shared:1) THEN make-slave → master:1.
- r305: `deploy/docker-root-slave.sh` (umount -l stale self-bind → bind from plain dir → make-slave; no-op when already slave), installed as `/usr/local/sbin/stoic-docker-root-slave` + docker.service drop-in `ExecStartPre=-…`. Bootstrap applies it right before step 4 (after checkout; stops dockerd ~20 s only when not slave; reset-failed + wait). Manual recovery given to user (stop docker → umount -l → bind → make-slave → start → alpine REMOVE OK).

## Host python / cPanel packman fix (June 2026 — DONE, user verification pending)
- Root cause: bootstrap.sh ran `alternatives --set python3 /usr/bin/python3.11` → cPanel packman
  (`#!/usr/bin/python3`, imports dnf) crashed (`No module named 'dnf'`); WHM MultiPHP then showed
  PHP-FPM "Unavailable" because it could not list packages.
- Fix: `deploy/host-python-fix.sh` (dry run / `--apply`) restores the stock /usr/bin/python3 via
  `alternatives --auto`, links python3.11 into private `/usr/local/lib/stoic/bin`; lib.sh + doctor/backup/
  healthwatch/install_report prepend that dir to PATH. bootstrap.sh never touches /usr/bin/python3 anymore
  and self-repairs old damage. doctor.sh fails when system python lacks dnf. Docs: DEPLOYMENT.md.
- User must run on host: `sudo bash deploy/host-python-fix.sh --apply`, then re-check WHM MultiPHP.

## overlay2 EBUSY — TRUE root cause: cPanel VirtFS (June 2026 — DONE, user verification pending)
- Alma 8.10 / 4.18: foreign-namespace copies cannot block rmdir (is_local_mountpoint); host-mount-fix.sh
  (MountFlags=slave on php-fpm etc.) was NOT the cause — do not restart cPanel services again (shared host).
- Cause: VirtFS bind-mounts /var/lib into /home/virtfs/<user>/ (15 jails) → container rootfs mounts copied
  INTO THE HOST NAMESPACE; 743 merged mounts for 10 containers; copies of dead layers share the dentry → EBUSY.
- Fix: lib.sh `orphan_merged_copies` / `detach_orphan_copies` (copies only, no shared peers, no docker-root
  paths, nothing when dockerd down) run first in reap_zombies; zombie removal unmounts all host paths of the
  layer. Standalone `deploy/docker-orphan-mounts.sh [--apply]`; doctor.sh fails while orphans exist.
- host-mount-fix.sh now classifies copies shared/private/slave.

## Session logout after ~30 min — FIXED (June 2026, user verification pending)
- Cause: refresh-token rotation reuse detection. Two tabs' access tokens (30 min) expire together → both
  POST /auth/refresh with the same cookie → second one is "reuse" → whole family revoked → logged out.
- Fix (security.py): consume is atomic (find_one_and_update publishes replaced_by_jti); a consumed token
  re-presented with the same hash within REFRESH_REUSE_GRACE_SECONDS (60) continues the chain from the
  newest successor; forged/late replays still revoke the family. Tests: tests/test_refresh_rotation_grace.py
  (incl. 4-way concurrent), test_iter57 replay test updated. Docs: CLOUDFLARE_EDGE.md, .env.example.
- Security audit (this session): CONDITIONAL PASS, no Critical/High; SEC-001 LOW open + 4 P3 deploy-script
  hardening items (env-inherited onboarding policy, PATH-prepend perms assert, mount sweep scoped to docker
  root ids, eval in host-python-fix.sh). User decision pending.

## A13 AUDIT FIX LIST — PART 2 (before live / real money) — DONE (2026-06, this fork; testing agent iteration_232 green, 11 unit + 8 HTTP)
- P0-01 crypto execution truth: `crypto_bridge/crypto_execution.py` — durable ExecutionIntent (source=crypto) + deterministic `client_order_id` ("stoic-<ulid>") BEFORE any exchange call; `_engine_stage` order path = cx.begin → create_*_order(client_order_id=) → cx.record_outcome (acked/filled/rejected) / cx.record_failure (ccxt ExchangeError → rejected; NetworkError/timeout → UNKNOWN, never resent, critical alert `crypto_execution_unknown`) → cx.protect (Binance spot OCO via `CCXTClient.place_oco_protection`; failure → fail-closed market flatten `<cid>-flat`, trade closed `protection_failed_flattened`, alert `crypto_protection_failed`; flatten failure → protection MISSING). `reconcile_all` (in `_scalp_reconcile_loop`, 45 s cadence) resolves dispatched/unknown/acked intents BY client order id (found → recorded exactly once, unique partial index `uniq_client_order_id`; not found past 60 s → failed_confirmed), re-protects/flattens unprotected opens, snapshots balances. Authority domain `crypto_protection` (account_bound) → CLOSE_ONLY while any open crypto position lacks exchange-side protection.
- P1-02 account-wide reservation: `account_reservations.py` — `guard_entry` wraps MT5 (`execute_authorized` → `_engine_stage`), Paper (`execute` → `_engine_stage`) and Crypto engines: present-or-reserve BEFORE the stage, caps (config auto cap, hard total = cap+EXEC_TOTAL_POSITIONS_BUFFER, `trade_of_day_cap` daily capacity, reserved risk) checked under a per-account lease (`account_reservation_locks`, 5 s TTL — a Mongo transaction alone does not serialize count-then-insert under snapshot isolation), rows in `risk_reservations` (scalp state machine; `source`, `caps_snapshot`, `capacity_snapshot`), released on refusal/error, SLOT_LINKED on insert; scalp presents its own reservation via `signal._reservation_id` (owns lifecycle). Old `count_documents` cap blocks removed from all engine stages; `trades.reservation_id` stamped.
- P1-01 acceptance bundle: `acceptance_bundle.py` — per enabled non-paper account: environment (DEMO via broker_env.demo_proof / LIVE), EA session freshness (≤5 min), reconciliation (≤24 h), positions, executions 24 h, unknown intents, bots, authority blockers (acceptance domain excluded), broker statement comparison (reconciliation_ledger, tolerance 0.01). HMAC-SHA256(LEDGER_ANCHOR_KEY) over the canonical payload, bound to build_sha + STOIC_IMAGE_DIGEST, 7-day TTL, stored `acceptance_bundles`. Authority domain `acceptance` (account_bound): LIVE accounts CLOSE_ONLY in production (or ACCEPTANCE_BUNDLE_REQUIRED=true) until a PASS bundle covers them for the current build/digest; DEMO/paper unaffected. Admin API: GET /admin/acceptance/current, POST /admin/acceptance/bundle (audit-chained), GET /admin/acceptance/bundles, GET /admin/release-gate. UI: `AcceptanceBundleCard.jsx` (section 04 on /admin/demo-readiness).
- P0-02 release gate: `release_gate.evaluate` (rc_lock authoritative + image digests + STOIC_IMAGE_DIGEST == locked backend digest + signed EX5 record) → authority domain `release_gate` (platform_global, production only, code RELEASE_NOT_AUTHORITATIVE); deploy_preflight check `release_gate` (fail in production / warn elsewhere); `generate_release_summary` verdict is now RELEASABLE only with authoritative lock + both digests + signed EX5 (else NOT RELEASABLE).
- Tests: tests/test_a13_part2.py (11), test_iter35_binance_ccxt.py updated to the new contract (client_order_id kwarg, OCO mock, ccxt error classes, `_mock_db` with real FakeCollections), unit/test_authority_matrix DOMAINS +3, agent's tests/test_iter232_a13_part2_http.py (8). Manifest regenerated.
- NOT in scope / still open: P1-05 reconciled performance evidence (labels simulated/indicative/broker-reconciled on charts) — queued as its own task; Part 3 (P2-01..04). Production still needs the real release workflow run (signed tag → authoritative lock, digests, signed EX5) and BRIDGE_TOKEN_HASH_KEY.

## 2026-10-07 — SECURITY TELEGRAM KEYS VIA ADMIN → INTEGRATIONS (vault) + I2 false-positive fix (unit lane 1070 green · preview E2E: set → status vault → test alert delivered → cleared)
- ROOT CAUSE (prod): the bot token was a Docker secret mounted ONLY into worker-security (S14), so the API (status card, SEND TEST ALERT) and worker-reconciliation (pairing / policy-expiry / demo-mode Telegram pushes) never had it → card always "not configured" on www.stoicaibot.com.
- FIX: `SECURITY_AGENT_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` (secret) + `SECURITY_AGENT_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID` are vault keys (`integrations_settings.REGISTRY`, provider `security_telegram`), set with the existing SET dialog (re-auth, sealed AES-256-GCM, audit chain). `VALIDATORS` reject a `bot` prefix / non-numeric chat id (422). `refresh_keys(db)` re-reads the two keys every evaluator cycle (`alerting.evaluate_ops_alerts`, `security_agent.runner.tick`) → workers follow set/clear without restart; cleared → falls back to .env / `*_FILE`. `test_provider("security_telegram")` = send_test (rate-limited 3/10 min). `security_alert_test.status` reports `token_source: vault`; `demo_readiness.env_checks(vault_token=…)` passes on the vault. UI: `SecurityAlertsCard` now hosts the two `KeyRow`s (new `components/admin/KeyRow.jsx`), reloads status after a key change; `AdminIntegrations` passes `keysOf(data, "security_telegram")`.
- I2 false positive: `REQUIRED_INDEXES["accounts"]` → `bridge_token_hash_1` (bridge tokens are hashed since A13-2; `bridge_token_1` is deliberately dropped) — this was the "Required unique index missing" Critical repeating every 30 min from the preview (197×) and would have fired in production too. Auto-resolves on the next check.
- PREVIEW: `SECURITY_AGENT_TELEGRAM_*` removed from backend/.env (preview no longer posts into the operator's chat); preview finding `agent_test_alert` resolved. Manual E2E script `tests/manual/security_telegram_vault_e2e.py` (creds from env).
- Tests: tests/unit/test_security_telegram_vault.py (3); test_admin_integrations / test_alert_test_and_scalp_migration adjusted. Manifest 5344 / 558 + rc_lock regenerated.
- OPERATOR: Save to GitHub → merge PR → `cd /opt/stoic && UPDATE_HOLD_ON_FAILURE=1 STOIC_READINESS_POLICY=onboarding-close-only deploy/update.sh origin/main` → Admin → Integrations → Security alerts (Telegram) → SET token + SET chat id 981306515 → SEND TEST ALERT. Then optionally empty `secrets/security_telegram_token` (it may still hold the literal `<FULL_TOKEN>` placeholder; the vault wins anyway).

## 2026-10-07 — CI `ea-release` FAILURE ROOT CAUSE + signer diagnosis (unit lane green · manifest + rc_lock regenerated)
- Error "external signer signature failed local verification against the pinned public key" (run 11). Probed the live Fly signer: `POST https://stoic-signer.fly.dev/sign {}` → 422 listing only `key_id`/`data_hex` — the deployed `app.py` is PRE-N100-11 (no `purpose`, signs RAW bytes without the `stoic:ea-release:v1\0` domain prefix). The client verifies WITH the prefix → always fails. The reviewer's "key changed" theory is wrong: the 1.60 record verifies (raw format) against the key Fly serves today (GET /public-key — the Ed25519 PUBLIC key, recorded in docs/PRODUCTION_DEPLOY_CHECKLIST.md); the checklist's previous value was a documentation error (fixed).
- OPERATOR: `cd deploy/signer && flyctl deploy -a stoic-signer` (code only — NEVER init_fly_signer.sh), confirm GitHub secret `RELEASE_PUBLIC_KEY_B64` == the value GET /public-key serves, re-run ea-release.

Tests: tests/unit/test_signer_verification_diagnosis.py (6).

## 2026-10-08 — Security audit #12 (code since #11 + black-box on www.stoicaibot.com and the Fly signer): PASS, no P0–P2 → all 5 P3s hardened (unit lane 1123 green)
- Auditor verified: VPS agent endpoints 401/403/405 never 500; agent tokens hashed; pairing tokens 192-bit single-use atomic; `terminal_login` 409 is NOT an oracle (only a holder of that account's token learns its own number); release-signing diagnosis exposes public keys only; signer POST /sign 401/403; CORS does not reflect hostile origins on credentialed routes; Turnstile on login. New VPS/ops endpoints not yet in prod (404) → verified on preview.
- P3-1 command params unsigned → `vps_pathb.canonical_params()` (ordinal keys, `key\0value\n`, escaping-free) + `params_sha256` + `sig_v2 = HMAC(agent_id|command_id|seq|command|params_sha256)` (legacy `sig` kept for the old host agent); poll delivers both; STOIC-Agent.ps1 REQUIRES `sig_v2` and recomputes the hash with `ConvertTo-StoicCanonicalParams` — byte-equality with Python proven by a pwsh 7.4.6 test (pwsh installed in the preview at /usr/local/bin/pwsh; all three .ps1 scripts parse).
- P3-2 enrolment: code widened to `ABCD-1234` (~4.6 B) and `/api/infra/agent/register` rate-limited 20/10 min per IP (live: 401 on a bogus code, no 500).
- P3-3 `POST/DELETE /api/ops/alerts/mute` now `_ops_admin_step_up` (TOTP step-up; frontend interceptor handles the 403 → prompt → retry); live anon → 403.

## 2026-10-08 — Release #7 (v1.60.4) install-from-archive ROOT CAUSE FOUND + FIXED → next tag v1.60.5 (unit lane 1146 green · manifest 5425/569 · rc_lock regenerated)
- Reproduced locally by running deploy/install.sh --dev with a fake docker shim (yields the exact backend/.env + secrets the installer writes) and booting `uvicorn server:app` under the compose container env. Crash: `security.py:390 REFRESH_REUSE_GRACE_SECONDS = int(os.environ.get(..., "60"))` → `ValueError: invalid literal for int(): ''`.
- ROOT CAUSE: the installer copies deploy/env/backend.env.example (≈80 blank `KEY=` lines) to backend/.env; `docker compose env_file` AND python-dotenv (no .dockerignore → backend/.env is baked into the image) both export those as KEY="" → every `int(os.environ.get(K, default))` dies at import → container never serves /api/health → unhealthy.
- FIX (backend/secrets_loader.py): `drop_blank_env()` — blank env values are UNSET; called first in `resolve_file_secrets()`. server.py + workers/base.py now call `resolve_file_secrets()` BEFORE the vault overlay (in Docker MONGO_URL only exists after *_FILE resolution → the sealed vault was silently skipped at boot). Verified: backend healthy in 6 s and worker boots under the simulated env.
- WHY NO LOG: `docker compose ps -a --format '{{.Name}} {{.State}} {{.Health}}'` printed nothing on the runner → app_boot_failure now enumerates `docker compose ps -aq` + `docker inspect`; compose_up dumps `compose ps -a` + backend logs after 6 attempts; release.yml install-from-archive gets a `Container logs on failure` step. Dockerfile.backend HEALTHCHECK: interval 10 s, start-period 120 s.
- Tests: backend/tests/unit/test_blank_env_boot.py (+5, incl. subprocess import of security.py with every template key blank).
- Pending operator: create GitHub secret RELEASE_EVIDENCE_KEY (hermetic-verify, REQUIRE_SIGNED_RESULTS=1); cut v1.60.5; then `deploy/update.sh v1.60.5` on the server.
